# The journey as a text adventure (learner child)

Everything the game said and everything the child did, level by level, in play order. Times are game seconds.

## Ninja Training (tutorial)

   0.0s SENSEI: Ninja training! First, tap the big gong!
   0.1s   --- tut-gong ---
   0.1s   > child taps: gong
   1.8s   [gong]
   1.8s SENSEI: Stuck? Tap me, down here in the corner. Try it now!
   2.6s   --- tut-help ---
   2.6s   [great]
   2.6s   > child taps: Help
   2.6s SENSEI: That's it! I'm always here to help.
   5.0s   > child taps: Help
   5.2s SENSEI: And when you tap the speaker, I'll say it again!
   6.2s   --- tut-speaker ---
   6.2s   [good]
   6.2s SENSEI: And when you tap the speaker, I'll say it again!
   6.2s   > child taps: Hear it again
   8.6s   > child taps: Hear it again
   9.1s SENSEI: Welcome to Bamboo Village!
  11.2s SENSEI: Tap the glowing stone to start your next adventure.

## Show Sensei (placement)

   0.0s SENSEI: Let's see what you know! Just have a go.
   0.1s   --- find ---
   0.3s   > child taps: o
   1.5s   [good]
   2.2s SENSEI: Which is the spelling of this sound?
   2.6s SENSEI: Which is the spelling of this sound?
   2.7s   [wrong]
   2.7s   > child taps: o
   3.4s   [fanfare]
   3.5s SENSEI: Hooray! We'll start right at the beginning.
   4.3s   [great]
   6.2s SENSEI: Welcome to Bamboo Village!
   8.3s SENSEI: Tap the glowing stone to start your next adventure.

## Bamboo Village w1-wu1: ears

   0.0s   --- warmup ---
   0.2s SENSEI: Ninja ears on! Let's listen to some words.
   3.4s SENSEI: This is the sun.
   5.1s SENSEI: This is a sock.
   6.4s SENSEI: This is a cat.
   7.7s SENSEI: Let me show you!
   8.8s SENSEI: Tap the sun!
   9.8s   [good]
  10.3s SENSEI: Now you try!
  11.3s SENSEI: Tap the sock!
  12.2s   > child taps: sock
  12.2s   [good]
  12.8s SENSEI: Watch me first!
  14.2s SENSEI: I can say a word fast. Sun!
  16.2s SENSEI: Or I can say it slowly...
      🔊 "sun" (slowly)
  20.5s SENSEI: Fast or slow, it's the same word. Sun!
  23.8s SENSEI: Slowly, I hear its sounds. Words are made of sounds!
  28.3s SENSEI: Your turn! Tap the tortoise, and say it slowly with me.
  29.2s   > child taps: tortoise
      🔊 "sun" (slowly)
  31.9s SENSEI: Now tap the rabbit, and say it fast!
  32.8s   > child taps: sun
      🔊 "sun" (slowly)
  35.3s SENSEI: Now tap the rabbit, and say it fast!
  37.7s   > child taps: rabbit
      🔊 "sun"
  38.6s SENSEI: Listen to the very first sound.
      🔊 "sun" (first sound held) · "sock" (first sound held)
  43.2s SENSEI: Did you notice? Sun and sock start with the same sound...
      🔊 /s/
  48.4s SENSEI: Say that sound with me!
      🔊 /s/
  52.3s SENSEI: This is a sausage.
  54.2s SENSEI: This is the moon.
  56.0s SENSEI: Let me show you!
  57.1s SENSEI: Tap all the pictures that start with...
      🔊 /s/
  60.8s   [good]
      🔊 "sun" (first sound held)
  62.1s SENSEI: Your turn!
  63.1s   > child taps: sock
  63.1s   [good]
      🔊 "sock" (first sound held)
  65.6s   > child taps: sun
  69.2s   > child taps: sausage
  69.2s   [good]
      🔊 "sausage" (first sound held)
  71.4s SENSEI: You found them both! They both start with...
      🔊 /s/
  75.4s   [great]
  75.4s SENSEI: You can hear the sounds in words. Brilliant listening!
  76.8s   [great]
  79.2s   [gong]
  79.2s SENSEI: Look! Your pictures are turning into stickers!
  80.0s   --- stickers ---
  83.0s SENSEI: This is your Sticker Book!
  84.4s SENSEI: Sun, sock, cat, sausage and moon!
  89.4s SENSEI: Every picture you play with becomes a sticker!
  92.1s SENSEI: Tap a sticker!
  92.2s   > child taps: sticker sun
      🔊 "sun" · "sun" (slowly) · "sun" · "sun" (slowly)
  94.5s SENSEI: Tap the green arrow to carry on!
